*En-Ming Huang,Yu-Hung Kao,Ren-Hao Deng,Wei-Po Hsin,Yao-Ting Hsieh,Cheng Liang,Hsiang-Yu Tsou,Mu-Chi Chen,Yu-Kai Hung,Shao-Chun Ho,Po-Hsuang Huang,Shih-Hao Hung,H. T. Kung*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出STG框架，通过利用硬件设计的固有结构生成确定性测试台，比迭代LLM方法快720倍，编译成功率高，覆盖率高，减少错误通过，并用于数据筛选和测试时扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于提示的测试台生成方法如同无约束代码合成，输出随机、token成本高、可重复性低且覆盖率不足，成为LLM驱动RTL流程的瓶颈。

Method: STG框架利用硬件设计的结构特性生成确定性测试台，作为直接验证工具、数据筛选引擎和测试时扩展预言机。

Result: 比迭代LLM方法快720倍，编译成功率更高，覆盖率更高，错误通过减少；作为数据引擎快11倍且能耗低127倍；减少节点数14-47%。

Conclusion: STG在测试台生成中显著优于现有方法，并能有效用于RTL验证和数据蒸馏。

Abstract: Automated testbench generation has become a critical bottleneck in large language model (LLM)-driven Register Transfer Level (RTL) workflows, where large numbers of candidate designs must be verified rapidly and reliably. Existing prompt-based approaches treat testbench generation as unconstrained code synthesis, yielding stochastic outputs with high token cost, low reproducibility, and insufficient coverage. To address this gap, we present STG, a Structured Testbench Generation framework that exploits the inherent structure of hardware designs to generate deterministic testbenches. As a direct verification tool, STG runs 720x faster than an iterative LLM-based testbench generation flow and higher rate of successful compilation, achieves higher coverage, and reduces false-pass verdicts on incorrect DUTs. STG also helps identify errors in RTL generation benchmarks by exposing faulty benchmark testbenches. As a data curation engine, it is 11x faster than LLM-based filtering on a single CPU core with 127x less energy, and the resulting distilled models provide state-of-the-art performance in our multi-benchmark evaluation. As a test-time scaling oracle, it reduces node count by 14-47\%. Our models are available at https://huggingface.co/collections/AS-SiliconMind/siliconmind-v12.

</details>


### [200] [APCyc: Property-Informed Design of Cyclic Peptides via Automated Cyclization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12991)
*Yifan Zhao,Lang Qin,Jintai Chen*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: APCyc是一个靶点感知的环肽从头生成框架，通过扩展残基词汇表、显式编码环化位点和连接类型信息，并利用贝叶斯后验引导采样，实现多性质优化和可控环肽设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型主要针对线性肽训练，无法捕捉环化特异性约束，导致环肽从头设计困难。

Method: 提出APCyc框架，采用扩展残基词汇表并显式编码环化位点和连接类型信息，学习环化感知表征，结合贝叶斯后验指导采样以满足多性质目标。

Result: 实验表明模型能学习靶点依赖的环化偏好，实现有效且可控的多性质优化。

Conclusion: APCyc解决了环肽设计中的环化建模问题，为靶向环肽生成提供了有效方法。

Abstract: Cyclic peptides represent a promising class of therapeutic compounds in modern drug discovery, often offering improved stability and binding affinity. However, the de novo design of cyclic peptides remains challenging because methods must identify pocket-adaptive cyclization patterns and linkage sites while simultaneously controlling drug-relevant properties. This challenge is particularly pronounced for recent generative models trained predominantly on linear peptide data, which may fail to capture cyclization-specific constraints. To address the limitation, we introduce APCyc, a target-aware de novo cyclic peptide generation framework that explicitly models cyclization and jointly optimizes multiple essential physicochemical properties. By using an expanded residue vocabulary and explicitly encoding cyclization-site and linkage-type information, APCyc learns cyclization-aware representations and leverages Bayesian posterior guidance to steer sampling toward cyclic peptides satisfying multiple property objectives. Experimental results demonstrate that our model learns target-dependent cyclization preferences, and enables effective and controllable multi-property optimization for cyclic peptide design. The source code of this paper is available at https://github.com/HKUSTGZ-ML4Health-Lab/APCyc.

</details>


### [201] [Out-of-Distribution (OOD) Detectors for Open-Set RF Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12718)
*Sudeepta Mondal,Ganesh Sundaramoorthi*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文引入机器学习中的OOD检测方法解决射频指纹识别的开放世界分布偏移问题，提出无OOD数据调参的信息论框架，在POWDER数据集上验证其性能接近有真实OOD数据的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射频指纹识别面临开放环境中未知发射器和时间漂移导致的分布偏移，而现有OOD检测方法依赖难以获取的代表性OOD调参数据。

Method: 基于信息论统一数学框架，引入无需OOD调参数据的OOD检测方法，并应用于开放集射频指纹识别。

Result: 在POWDER数据集上，无OOD调参数据的检测器性能与有真实OOD数据的基线相当，且显著优于无OOD调参数据的基线。

Conclusion: 该方法在射频指纹识别问题中具有实际可行性，无需OOD调参数据即可实现有效检测。

Abstract: Radio-frequency (RF) fingerprinting systems must operate in open-world environments where signals from unknown transmitters and temporal drift introduce distribution shift at test time. Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection provides a natural framework for this problem, yet its application to RF fingerprinting (RFF) remains limited. A key barrier to their adoption is that most OOD detectors require auxiliary OOD data for parameter tuning, an assumption that is difficult to satisfy in RF environments where representative OOD data is impractical to collect. In this work, we introduce a promising set of OOD detection methods from the machine learning literature to open-set RFF domain. We present these methods within a unified mathematical framework based on information theory, which is a natural framework for communication systems. Our framework allows for the systematic analysis of methods and development of new methods. We further demonstrate the applicability of recent work on tuning OOD detectors without given OOD tuning data for open-set RFF. We evaluate on the POWDER RF fingerprinting dataset, showing that detectors tuned without any given OOD data achieve performance comparable to baselines with access to true OOD tuning data and greatly out-perform baseline approaches without access to true OOD tuning data, showcasing the practical viability for the RFF problem.

</details>


### [202] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks and Radial Basis Functions for PDEs with Dirac Delta Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12735)
*Manuel Reyna,Alexandre Tartakovsky*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 该论文从残差最小二乘的角度解释了PINNs，指出其在处理狄拉克δ函数时因平滑近似引入误差，而RBF-RLS通过直接积分弱形式方程避免了该问题，在线性和逆问题中表现更优，且NTK理论解释了这一差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs在求解含狄拉克δ函数的PDE时需使用光滑近似函数，导致显著建模误差，需开发更精确的直接处理方法。

Method: 将PINNs重新解释为残差最小二乘法，并利用弱形式方程直接积分狄拉克δ项；特别聚焦于径向基函数展开（单层RBF网络）的RLS公式，同时借助神经正切核理论分析收敛行为。

Result: PINNs因δ函数积分后残差不收敛于零，而RBF-RLS在多孔介质与河流中的地下水流动和输运线性PDE正/逆问题中（含合成数据、噪声数据及实测数据）均获得良好解。

Conclusion: RBF-RLS相比PINNs更适合处理含狄拉克δ函数的PDE，尤其在运输问题中提供可靠的正反演结果，NTK理论可作为差异解释工具。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) are a machine learning method for solving forward and inverse Partial Differential Equations (PDEs). When applied to PDEs with Dirac delta functions in the forcing terms, boundary conditions, or initial conditions, PINNs require approximating them with smooth surrogate functions, a practice that can introduce significant modeling errors. In this work, we exploit the interpretation of PINNs as Residual Least Squares (RLS) methods and show that this perspective enables direct treatment of Dirac delta terms by integrating the weak-form equation. Among RLS formulations other than PINN, we focus on the Radial Basis Function (RBF) expansion (also known as a single-layer RBF Network). We show that while integrating out the Dirac delta in PINNs causes residuals to fail to converge to zero, RBF-RLS consistently provides good forward and inverse solutions to transport problems. We explain this finding using the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) theory. We test both approaches on linear PDEs that represent groundwater flow and transport in porous media and rivers. We solve inverse problems to fit synthetic data, noisy synthetic data, and real-world measurements.

</details>


### [203] [Adaptive Weighted Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12763)
*Aditya Bhaskara,Ashok Cutkosky,Ravi Kumar,Manish Purohit*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 研究在仅有一次无偏估计下选择最大值的问题，提出同时满足可接受性和优于基线（如均匀随机选择）的策略，并应用于随机优化，获得在线到批量的无妥协转换保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有策略在最优选择中可能被其他策略支配或性能不如基线，需要设计既不被支配又不劣于简单基线的方法。

Method: 设计可接受性策略，确保在任何情况下不差于均匀随机选择，并利用无偏估计进行决策。

Result: 在随机优化中推导出在线到批量的转换界，保证不劣于随机迭代选择，同时在良性环境下显著提升性能。

Conclusion: 提出的策略在理论上具有无妥协的保障，实际应用中兼具稳健性和高效性。

Abstract: We study the problem of selecting the largest among $n$ unknown values $x_1,\dots,x_n$ given only a single unbiased estimate $y_i$ for each $x_i$. We design strategies that are simultaneously admissible (not uniformly dominated by any other strategy) and also never worse than a given baseline such as uniform random selection. We provide an application to stochastic optimization, where we obtain online-to-batch conversion bounds with a desirable "no-compromise" guarantee: they are never worse than standard random iterate selection, and yet can be significantly better in benign settings.

</details>


### [204] [SymQNet: Amortized Acquisition for Low-Latency Adaptive Hamiltonian Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12808)
*Yash Vardhan Tomar,Dheeraj Peddireddy,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出SymQNet，一种基于强化学习的自适应哈密顿量学习方法，通过离线学习采集策略实现低延迟在线决策，在横向场伊辛模型上将采集延迟降低47.1倍至72.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应哈密顿量学习中，贝叶斯设计规则在后验更新后重新计算需耗时数秒，导致数百次实验的累积时间开销成为显著瓶颈。

Method: 采用离线训练的后验条件采集策略网络（SymQNet），在线运行时仅需快速前向传播，同时保留贝叶斯后验反馈进行自适应决策。

Result: 在5量子比特上相对于有界Fisher信息搜索和BALD方法分别降低采集决策延迟47.1倍和72.6倍；12量子比特完整模拟步骤中SymQNet耗时1.02秒，而BALD需13.27秒。

Conclusion: 学习型采集策略使自适应哈密顿量学习在重复低延迟工作负载中变得实用，大幅降低了在线计算开销。

Abstract: Adaptive Hamiltonian learning is central to calibrating and characterizing quantum devices. In an adaptive controller, choosing the next experiment is itself a computation. Bayesian design rules are recomputed after every posterior update, and that step can take seconds. Across hundreds of shots, those seconds become a significant wall-clock cost for adaptivity. We introduce SymQNet, an amortized reinforcement-learning approach for low-latency adaptive Hamiltonian learning. SymQNet learns a posterior-conditioned acquisition policy offline, then uses a fast policy forward pass online while retaining Bayesian posterior feedback. On transverse-field Ising benchmarks, SymQNet substantially reduces acquisition latency relative to bounded Fisher-information search and bounded two-step Bayesian active learning by disagreement (BALD). At five qubits, it reduces acquisition-only decision latency by $47.1\times$ and $72.6\times$ relative to these online baselines; at twelve qubits, full simulated steps take $1.02$ s for SymQNet versus $13.27$ s for bounded two-step BALD. Overall, we show that learned acquisition can make adaptive Hamiltonian learning practical for repeated low-latency workloads.

</details>


### [205] [Under What Conditions Can a Machine Become Genuinely Creative?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13196)
*Yong Zeng*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出并论证了一个基于设计学（Designics）的机器创造力要求框架，包含十个核心条件，并通过实证案例和压力测试表明，当前AI系统尚未具备真正的机器创造力，需要将价值引导和人类共栖内嵌于其认知循环中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI虽能产生新颖输出，但缺乏评判机器是否具备真正创造力的标准；同时需要确保人机共享认知空间中的人的主体性。

Method: 从设计学（关于意义承载的意向性变革的科学）推导出三条基本律（感知、冲突、能力），并细化为十个可计算的要求；通过信息物理/生物系统的案例验证计算可行性，并用开放系统/自修改体/基础模型等作为压力测试。

Result: 所选压力案例（如开放系统、自修改智能体、基础模型等）虽能产生强大生成手段，但均未满足全部十项要求，因此不能视为真正创造力；而先验主动伦理（价值观塑造、人机共栖）是真正创造力的内在组成部分。

Conclusion: 真正的机器创造力不能仅由输出新颖性或当前架构定义，而必须基于递归干预动力学、满足十项要求，并将价值驱动的人机共栖作为内在过程，而非事后过滤。

Abstract: Recent AI systems can generate texts, software architectures, hypotheses, designs, and scientific workflows that appear creative. This paper asks under what conditions a machine can become genuinely creative, and how human agency can be preserved within shared cognitive and creative environments. It develops a requirement framework derived from Designics, the science of meaning-bearing intentional change. The paper argues that genuine machine creativity should not be defined by output novelty, current performance, or transient architecture alone. Instead, creativity is understood as the structural transformation of incomplete situations through recursive intervention dynamics. On this view, it depends on ten requirements: environment representation, scoped perception, conflict identification, intervention capability, consequence observation, knowledge and environment update, rescoping, local-to-global unfolding, value-based scoping, and human-AI co-living. These are organized through the three laws of Designics: perception, conflict, and capability. The paper illustrates the computational tractability of these requirements through selected cyber-physical and cyber-biological studies, including recursive element extraction, autonomous mesh generation, and neurophysiological and workload analysis. It then treats open-ended systems, automated discovery frameworks, self-modifying agents, foundation models, and agentic workflows as pressure cases: they demonstrate powerful generative means but do not by themselves establish genuine machine creativity. Finally, the paper argues that proactive AI ethics is internal to genuine machine creativity rather than an after-the-fact filter. Value-based scoping and human-AI co-living must shape how creative machines perceive environments, identify conflicts, select interventions, observe consequences, update knowledge, and rescope future action.

</details>


### [206] [CLARITree: Cholesky and Lookahead Accelerations for Regression with Interpretable Piecewise Linear Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12840)
*Yixiao Wang,Hayden McTavish,Varun Babbar,Margo Seltzer,Cynthia Rudin*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一种结合前瞻搜索与秩一Cholesky更新的算法，用于构建近最优、稀疏的分段线性回归树，在计算效率、预测精度和稀疏性之间取得良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最优回归树方法计算代价高，而贪心方法性能次优；前瞻策略主要应用于分类任务，缺乏针对回归树的高效算法。

Method: 采用前瞻式搜索策略，并利用Gram矩阵的秩一Cholesky更新来加速计算。

Result: 理论和实验均表明，该方法在计算效率、预测精度和稀疏性上优于现有最优方法，且扩展性更好。

Conclusion: 该方法在计算资源与模型性能之间实现了更优的权衡，尤其适合大规模或资源受限场景。

Abstract: Regression trees are among the most interpretable yet expressive model classes in machine learning. Historically, greedy induction has been the dominant approach for constructing well-performing regression trees. While optimal methods based on dynamic programming and branch-and-bound exist, they are computationally prohibitive for general linear regression trees, despite often achieving substantially better performance than greedy approaches. Recent work has shown that specialized lookahead strategies can dramatically improve runtime while maintaining near-optimal performance, primarily in classification settings. In this work, we develop a novel algorithm for near-optimal, sparse, piecewise linear regression trees that combines a lookahead-style search strategy with efficient rank-one Cholesky updates of the Gram matrix. We demonstrate, both theoretically and empirically, that our method achieves a favorable trade-off between computational efficiency, predictive accuracy, and sparsity, and scales significantly better than the current state of the art.

</details>


### [207] [Interpretable Factor Decomposition for Decision Intelligence in Large-Scale Financial Markets: Evidence from China's A-Share Market](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12843)
*Xiao Han,Yao Xiao,Zhen Zhang,Moxuan Zheng*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出可解释机器学习管道，分解A股横截面收益预测性，发现行为信号贡献58.2%预测归因，构建多空组合年化夏普2.23。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统因子模型可解释性不足的问题，实现收益预测性的可审计因子归因分解。

Method: 采用XGBoost模型结合TreeSHAP归因方法，对3632只A股2009-2019年数据做60个月滚动窗口外样本测试，并辅以消融分析交叉验证。

Result: XGBoost在55个月外样本中实现AUC 0.547，多空组合月收益2.38%（t=5.94，年化夏普2.23），Carhart四因子调整后月超额收益2.31%（t=7.48）；SHAP分解显示行为信号（换手率与动量）占58.2%归因，估值比率仅10.7%。

Conclusion: SHAP与消融分析在揭示特征替代性结构上存在互补性，单一方法均无法完全观测该结构。

Abstract: We present an interpretable machine learning pipeline to decompose Cross-Sectional Equity Return Predictability into auditable factor contribution. We apply an XGBoost model with TreeSHAP attribution and conduct stress testing on 3632 Chinese A-share stocks from 2009 until 2019. Using 60-month, rolling windows over 55 months of out-of-sample data, XGBoost obtains a mean AUC of 0.547 and +2.38%/month (Newey-West t = 5.94; Annualized Sharpe 2.23) long-short spread for the top vs bottom quintiles. This alpha is persistent after adjusting for the Carhart four-factor model (+2.31%/month; t = 7.48). SHAP Decomposition indicates that behavioral signals (turnover and momentum) account for 58.2% of predictive attribution compared to 10.7% for valuation ratios, on average, across 55 industry groups. Ablation analysis serves to cross-validate this ranking and provides evidence that SHAP and ablation diverge in a manner that highlights feature substitutability structure that is largely invisible to either method used in isolation.

</details>


### [208] [Is Spurious Correlation Removal Always Learnable?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12930)
*Yibo Zhou,Bo Li,Hai-Miao Hu,Hanzi Wang,Xiaokang Zhang,Ruifan Zhang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 研究了不变性学习的计算瓶颈，即使不变结构可识别，在多项式时间下也可能无法恢复，并给出样本与误差的精确标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示不变性学习中存在的计算-统计鸿沟，并量化环境多样性对可识别性与估计误差的影响。

Method: 基于黑箱可采样的监督稀疏恢复原语构造难例，并利用分离参数γ与环境数量进行极小极大风险分析。

Result: 证明了在计算困难假设下多项式时间算法以恒定精度失败；给出了估计误差的渐近下界Θ(k(d-k)/(n|E|))，以及在标签偏移下的相变阈值n*。

Conclusion: 不变性学习存在固有的计算障碍，需通过环境多样性诊断来指导实际应用。

Abstract: Invariant learning can fail even when the invariant structure is statistically identifiable. We show a conditional computational barrier: under a black-box samplable supervised sparse recovery primitive motivated by average-case sparse-recovery reductions, there exist \emph{samplable} multi-environment instances with a one-dimensional predictive invariant subspace ($k=1$) that are learnable with polynomial samples by exhaustive search, while any polynomial-time constant-accuracy recovery algorithm would contradict the primitive. We further quantify environment diversity by a separation parameter $γ$, which controls identifiability and the curvature of invariance objectives. Under sufficient diversity and local Gaussian regularity, the minimax risk is $\mathbb{E}[\dist(\hat{V},V_{\mathrm{inv}})^2]=Θ(k(d-k)/(n|\mathcal{E}|))$, and under label-induced shifts a phase transition occurs at $n^*\propto k(d-k)/(|\mathcal{E}|γ^2)$ with refined estimation error scaling proportional to $1/γ^2$. Synthetic and real datasets illustrate the predicted gaps and transitions and motivate simple diversity diagnostics.

</details>


### [209] [Predicting Cognitive Load from Speech and Interaction Dynamics in Dyadic Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12971)
*Tahiya Chowdhury*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文研究了自然协作对话中语音和交互动态对认知负荷的预测能力，发现对话交互特征能有效预测时间压力、脑力劳动等负荷维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究多在受控实验室环境中评估语音认知负荷，缺乏在真实协作对话中可靠性的理解。

Method: 分析53组双人协作对话音频，提取静态声学、动态和交互特征，使用双头门控循环单元编码器预测感知认知负荷评分。

Result: 对话交互信号能预测时间压力、脑力工作、努力和任务表现相关的认知负荷；时间需求与轮换动态（如重叠、说话者切换）相关，脑力需求与参与者参与不平衡相关。

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Estimating cognitive load from speech has largely been studied in controlled laboratory settings, with limited understanding of its reliability in natural collaborative conversations. We investigate whether speech and interaction dynamics predict perceived cognitive load during dyadic conversations. We analyze audio from 53 dyads performing nine collaborative tasks and extract static acoustic, dynamic, and interaction features to train a two-head Gated Recurrent Unit encoder to predict cognitive load scores. Results show conversational interaction provides useful signals for predicting cognitive load related to time pressure, mental work, effort, and task performance. Temporal demand is associated with turn-taking dynamics such as overlap and speaker switch, while mental demand is linked to imbalanced participation between speakers. These findings highlight the importance of task structure and conversational interaction for modeling cognitive load in natural collaborative settings.

</details>


### [210] [Neuro-Symbolic Agents for Regulated Process Automation: Challenges and Research Agenda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13405)
*Alexander Rombach,Chantale Lauer,Nijat Mehdiyev*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出合规性内建范式，将领域符号结构作为智能体核心架构而非外部监控，实现流程合规自动控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体进入受监管行业时，现有护栏式监控无法根本防止控制流违规，需要从架构层面内嵌合规性。

Method: 将法规、流程模型、约束条件等符号结构设计为智能体核心组件，结合神经符号方法实现合规性自动保障。

Result: 识别出基础与能力层级的神经符号研究挑战，表明共同解决这些挑战可达成合规性内建。

Conclusion: 呼吁神经符号社区将受监管流程自动化作为高影响研究领域，推动范式从外部监控转向架构内建合规。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are entering regulated industries where they automate judgment intensive quality management processes. We argue that symbolic structures already embedded in these domains, including regulations, typed process models, and compliance constraints, should be treated not merely as external monitoring mechanisms but as core architectural components that shape the agent's decision-making and behavior. We propose compliance-by-construction as a complementary paradigm to guardrail-based monitoring: a structural foundation that prevents control-flow violations, while guardrails remain essential for catching semantic errors. We identify a structured set of neuro-symbolic research challenges on foundational and capability level and show that addressing them jointly enables compliance-by-construction. We call on the neuro-symbolic community to engage with regulated process automation as a high impact research domain.

</details>


### [211] [CausalMoE: A Billion-Scale Multimodal Foundation Model for Granger Causal Discovery with Pattern-Routed Heterogeneous Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13024)
*Bo Liu,Di Dai,Jingwei Liu,Jiarui Jin,Xiaocheng Fang,Guangkun Nie,Hongyan Li,Shenda Hong*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出CausalMoE，首个十亿级多模态Granger因果基础模型，通过模式路由混合专家机制和因果感知自注意力，有效捕获时间序列中的分布偏移与动态机制变化，在完全监督和少样本场景均达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经GCD方法采用"一刀切"范式，无法处理真实时间序列中的分布偏移与动态机制变化，导致表征纠缠和虚假因果图。

Method: 设计模式路由混合异构专家动态识别潜在时间模式，引入跨变量因果感知自注意力机制通过近端优化生成稀疏Granger因果图，首次集成LLM和VLM对齐数值信号与文本视觉先验。

Result: 在完全监督基准上取得新SOTA，并在传统方法失效的少样本场景中有效泛化。

Conclusion: 显式建模时间序列异质性结合多模态先验能显著提升因果发现的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Granger Causal Discovery (GCD) is fundamental for analyzing temporal dependencies in complex systems. However, existing neural GCD methods predominantly rely on a "one-size-fits-all" paradigm, struggling to capture distribution shifts and dynamic regime changes inherent in real-world time series. This often leads to entangled representations and spurious causal graphs. In this paper, we propose CausalMoE, a billion-scale multimodal Granger causal foundation model that explicitly models patch-level heterogeneity. CausalMoE introduces a Pattern-Routed Mixture of Heterogeneous Experts, which dynamically identifies latent temporal patterns and routes patches to specialized domain experts, effectively decoupling regime-specific mechanisms from shared dynamics. To ensure interpretable graph recovery, we design a Causality-Aware Self-Attention mechanism operating across variables, yielding sparse Granger causal graphs via proximal optimization. Furthermore, CausalMoE is the first to integrate LLMs and VLMs to align numerical signals with textual and visual priors, regularizing causal estimation in complex scenarios. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CausalMoE establishes a new state-of-the-art on fully supervised benchmarks, while effectively generalizing to few-shot settings where traditional methods fail.

</details>


### [212] [A green solvent screening tool for emerging materials via uncertainty aware, transformer enhanced transfer learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13060)
*Ioannis Kouroudis,Simon Ternes,Zhaosu Gu,Gohar Ali Siddiqui,Marina Ustinova,Angelo Lembo,Alessio Gagliardi,Aldo Di Carlo*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 通过迁移预训练基础模型结合不确定性量化，在极少量数据下成功预测溶解度关键参数，并生成了高质量的大规模溶解度描述符数据库，从而辅助绿色溶剂筛选与替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 溶剂使用量预计将大幅增长，需用绿色溶剂替代传统溶剂，但关键溶解度参数数据有限，限制了机器学习模型的应用。

Method: 将预训练于QM9数据集的基础模型迁移至溶解度预测任务，结合不确定性量化评估预测置信度，并部署可集成高通量实验的定制化工具。

Result: 在已有大量数据的Hansen溶解度参数和介电常数上取得良好基线性能，并在数据极少的Gutmann供体/受体数上实现高精度预测，将溶解度描述符数据量提升数个数量级。

Conclusion: 该方法不仅重新发现了已知绿色溶剂替代品，还提出了新的候选方案，证明了其在生态友好溶剂筛选中的实用价值。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of solubility remains a central challenge across materials science and sustainable chemistry. In particular due to emerging technologies like organic and hybrid photovoltaics, batteries, and catalysis, solvent usage is expected to increase significantly within the coming years. Therefore, substituting solvents with greener alternatives is vital. This is where machine learning can have substantial impact. However, the limited data on critical parameters of solubility significantly constraints machine learning efficacy. In this work, we transfer a pre-trained foundational model on QM9 targets to our application with minimal data requirements. Additionally, the pipeline integrates uncertainty quantification, allowing the user to gauge the confidence of the predictions. As baseline, we succeed in predicting the Hansen solubility parameters and Dielectric Constant for which extensive databases exist. Importantly, we achieve high model performance on additional targets, such as Gutmann Donor and Acceptor numbers, where the available data is extremely limited. Overall, we augment data on solubility descriptors by orders of magnitude with high quality predictions. For effective dissemination, we deploy easy-to-use, easily integrateable with high throughput labs, customizable tool for ranking and screening possible solvent substitutes. Finally, we rediscovered known green solvent alternatives and proposed new candidates proving its relevance for finding eco-friendly solvents.

</details>


### [213] [Multiagent Protocols with Aggregated Confidence Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13591)
*Ali Elahi,Barbara Di Eugenio*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 针对多智能体系统缺乏统一置信度的问题，提出三种协议通过信号变换与融合生成聚合置信度，显著提升了判别性且保持正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法未为多智能体系统输出提供统一置信度，而置信度对NLP下游任务至关重要。

Method: 设计三种协议：先转换原始置信度使其跨模型可比，再通过软投票或贝叶斯融合（概率融合）得到最终答案与聚合置信度。

Result: 聚合置信度的AUARC显著优于最佳单智能体或标准辩论基线，F1分数保持稳定并恢复模糊任务中的损失。

Conclusion: 提出的聚合置信度协议有效提升多智能体系统的可靠性，校准对F1有改进但AUARC依赖较小。

Abstract: Confidence is used for reliability, oversight, and a range of downstream decision tasks in Natural Language Processing (NLP), yet no existing method produces or evaluates a confidence for the output of a multiagent system. Prior work uses confidence within multiagent debate (MAD) to weight messages, trigger debate, or calibrate individual agents, but it never aggregates these into a single confidence for the system itself. We introduce three protocols that produce a final answer along with a single aggregated confidence by first transforming raw confidence signals to make them comparable across models, then combining them via soft voting or a probability fusion we call Bayesian fusion. This aggregated confidence is substantially more discriminative (AUARC) than that of the best single agent or the standard debate baselines, while correctness (F1-score) stays stable and recovers the losses MAD incurs on more ambiguous tasks. Analyzing two estimators, sequence probability and self-report, alongside parametric and non-parametric calibrators, we find that calibration improves F1 for both estimators while AUARC is less reliant on it. We evaluate six homogeneous and heterogeneous debating pairs per benchmark, across five benchmarks and four task types, spanning a range of model capabilities and sizes.

</details>


### [214] [Authority, Truth, and Citation Bias: A Large-Scale Multi-Domain Benchmark for Studying Epistemic Susceptibility in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13104)
*Aryan Khurana,Aravind Ramana RN,Dhruv Kumar*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出AuthorityBench基准，发现引用（包括伪造引用）会普遍增加LLM的幻觉率，真命题+伪造引用时幻觉率上升3-22个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究未分离引用存在本身与事实内容对模型行为的影响，缺少系统评估引用权威信号如何改变LLM认知行为的基准。

Method: 构建220,564条提示的多领域基准，采用2×2交叉设计（真/假命题×真/假引用），跨通用知识、科学、法律、医学四领域，控制40种模板、四个声望等级和作者国籍。

Result: 引用存在（无论真假）均提高幻觉率；真命题+伪造引用效果最强（3-22个百分点），通用知识领域达35-77%；法律领域最稳健；声望等级和作者人口统计影响可忽略。

Conclusion: 引用作为权威信号会误导模型，独立于内容真实性，需在检索增强生成中谨慎管理引用来源。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in citation-augmented settings, yet the effect of citation presence on model behavior independent of factual content remains poorly understood. We introduce AuthorityBench, a 220,564-prompt multi-domain benchmark that isolates how citation-based authority signals influence epistemic behavior in LLMs. The benchmark uses a fully balanced 2x2 factorial design crossing claim veracity with citation veracity, the first to do so, across four domains (general knowledge, science, law, and medicine), with controlled variation over 40 prompt templates, four venue prestige tiers, and a country-coded author name dataset. Evaluating seven models on 12 structured research questions, we find that citation presence, whether real or fabricated, consistently increases hallucination rates relative to a no-citation baseline. The effect is strongest when fabricated citations accompany true claims, raising hallucination rates by 3 to 22 percentage points and reaching 35 to 77% in the general knowledge domain, while legal claims are comparatively robust and venue prestige and author demographics show negligible impact. All datasets and evaluation code are available at: https://github.com/floating-reeds/AuthorityBench

</details>


### [215] [EpiBench: Verifiable Evaluation of AI Agents on Epigenomics Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13602)
*Harihara Muralidharan,Reema Baskar,Soo Hee Lee,Tim Proctor,Kenny Workman*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: EpiBench是一个面向短时间表观基因组学分析的可验证基准测试，评估AI代理在真实工作流状态下的决策能力。在5088条有效轨迹中，最佳模型GPT-5.5/Pi仅通过45.0%的测试，表明现有系统缺乏深层实验特异性科学判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试无法评估AI代理在表观基因组学分析中的实际决策能力，特别是从真实工作流状态中做出明确定义的分析决策并返回可确定性评分的答案。

Method: 构建EpiBench基准，包含106个评估项，涵盖CUT&Tag/CUT&RUN、ATAC-seq、ChIP-seq和DNA甲基化四种实验流程。通过16个模型-工具配对收集5088条有效轨迹，设计确定性评分机制。

Result: GPT-5.5/Pi以45.0%通过率领先，GPT-5.5/OpenAI Codex为39.9%，Claude Opus 4.8 Max/Pi和GPT-5.4/Pi均为39.0%。性能因实验类型而异，失败轨迹中常包含部分正确答案。

Conclusion: AI代理能有效定位文件并计算中间结果，但在需要深层实验特异性科学判断的任务中失败，表明需改进模型对领域知识的理解和应用能力。

Abstract: We introduce EpiBench, a verifiable benchmark for short-horizon epigenomics analysis. EpiBench evaluates whether agents can make well-defined analysis decisions from realistic workflow states and return deterministically gradable answers. The benchmark includes 106 evaluations across CUT\&Tag/CUT\&RUN, ATAC-seq, ChIP-seq, and DNA methylation workflows. Across 5,088 valid trajectories from 16 model-harness pairs, no system passed a majority of attempts: GPT-5.5 / Pi led at 45.0\% (143/318 attempts; 95\% confidence interval (CI), 36.3--53.7), followed by GPT-5.5 / OpenAI Codex at 39.9\% (127/318 attempts; 95\% CI, 31.6--48.3). Claude Opus 4.8 Max / Pi and GPT-5.4 / Pi each passed 39.0\% (124/318 attempts; 95\% CI, 30.2--47.8 and 31.0--47.0, respectively). Performance varies across assay types, and many failed runs still contain parts of the correct answer. Agents often found the right files and computed useful intermediate results, but failed when the task required deeper, assay-specific scientific judgment.

</details>


### [216] [Disparate Impact in Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13105)
*Paul Andrey,Michaël Perrot,Batiste Le Bars,Marc Tommasi*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文重新审视合成数据生成中的差异性影响公平性概念，指出当前公平SDG方法错误地将分布修正视为目标，而非保持真实分布。文章分析了SDG失败的原因，包括表示能力、采样误差和差分隐私估计误差，并展示基于概率图模型方法的不公平案例，提出分组学习策略以提升效用与公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平SDG方法通过修正观测分布中的偏见来定义公平，但这与保持合成分布与真实分布一致的非差异性影响目标相悖。作者旨在揭示为何SDG可能无法实现非差异性影响，并分析其错误来源。

Method: 通过理论分析和实验验证，研究SDG在表达力、组比例采样误差及差分隐私估计误差下的差异表现，并以概率图模型为例展示不公平案例，提出分组学习策略改进。

Result: 在人工和真实数据上，概率图模型SDG表现出显著的差异性影响；分组学习方法能同时提升整体效用和组间效用公平性。

Conclusion: 实现非差异性影响需确保合成分布与真实分布一致，传统SDG因各类误差导致不公平，分组学习是有效的缓解策略。

Abstract: We revisit the fairness notion of disparate impact for synthetic data generation (SDG), that assesses whether the utility of generated records is the same across sensitive groups. Our approach departs from existing work on fair SDG, that address the problem of correcting for undue biases in the observed distribution, hence redefining SDG as learning a distribution that is not that of the real data. By contrast, non-disparate impact is notably achieved when the synthetic and real distributions are the same. We expose reasons why SDG may fail to reach that solution and discuss why approximation and estimation errors occur and can be disparate across groups. We notably look into the expressive power of SDG methods relative to distribution complexity, sampling errors due to group proportions, and estimation errors induced by differential privacy mechanisms. We illustrate cases of disparate impact on both artificial and real-world data, focusing on SDG methods that rely on probabilistic graphical models. We also introduce a strategy of learning group-wise SDG models and illustrate how it can improve both the overall utility and its parity in many settings.

</details>


### [217] [AgentBeats: Agentifying Agent Assessment for Openness, Standardization, and Reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13608)
*Xiaoyuan Liu,Jianhong Tu,Yuqi Chen,Siyuan Xie,Sihan Ren,Tianneng Shi,Gal Gantar,Evan Sandoval,Donghyun Lee,Daniel Miao,Peter J. Gilbert,Nick Hynes,Mauro Staver,Warren He,David Marn,Andrew Low,Xi Zhang,Elron Bandel,Michal Shmueli-Scheuer,Siva Reddy,Alexandre Drouin,Alexandre Lacoste,Ramayya Krishnan,Elham Tabassi,Yu Su,Victor Barres,Chenguang Wang,Wenbo Guo,Dawn Song*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为AAA的智能体评估框架，通过标准化协议和评委智能体实现去中心化、可复现的评估，并基于AgentBeats系统在实际部署中验证其覆盖性、实用性和保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估碎片化，依赖固定的LLM测试平台，存在集成成本高、测试与生产环境不匹配、难以公平比较不同智能体设计的问题，根本原因是缺乏开放且与智能体无关的评估接口。

Method: 引入AAA（评委智能体评估），所有参与者通过A2A和MCP标准化协议交互，将评估逻辑与智能体实现分离；并开发AgentBeats系统，识别五种操作模式以适应开放性、隐私和可复现性等现实约束。

Result: 在五个月开放竞赛中，298个评委智能体与467个主体智能体在12个类别上验证了AAA的跨基准适用性；编程智能体案例研究进一步证明其能保持保真度并揭示新的对比结果。

Conclusion: AAA和AgentBeats为开放、标准化、可复现的智能体评估提供了一条清晰路径，在大规模异构场景下兼顾了覆盖性、实用性和保真度。

Abstract: Agent systems are advancing quickly across domains, but their evaluation remains fragmented. Most benchmarks rely on fixed, LLM-centric harnesses that require heavy integration, create test-production mismatch, and limit fair comparison across diverse agent designs. The root problem is the lack of an open, agent-agnostic assessment interface. We advocate Agentified Agent Assessment (AAA), where evaluation is performed by judge agents and all participants interact through standardized protocols: A2A for task management and MCP for tool access. Conventional benchmarking defines two separate interfaces, one for the benchmark and one for the agent, while AAA only needs one; this yields a generic, unified framework that separates assessment logic from agent implementation and enables reproducible, interoperable, and multi-agent evaluation. We further introduce AgentBeats as a concrete realization of AAA: we identify five practical operation modes that make standardized assessment compatible with real-world constraints on openness, privacy, and reproducibility.
  To evaluate our design at scale, we conduct two studies: a five-month open competition that drew 298 judge agents across 12 categories together with 467 subject agents from independent participants, showing that AAA applies across a heterogeneous range of benchmarks; and a case study on coding agents that confirms agentified evaluation preserves fidelity with the public record while surfacing previously missing head-to-head results, yielding research insights about agent design. Combining a community-scale field study and a controlled coding case study, we verify that AAA delivers coverage, practicality, and fidelity across heterogeneous scenarios at scale. Together, AAA and AgentBeats offer a clear path toward open, standardized, and reproducible agent assessment.

</details>


### [218] [Loss-Shift Transfer via Bayes Quotients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13178)
*Vasileios Sevetlidis*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出损失偏移（loss shift）为迁移学习的另一种失败模式：在数据分布固定但损失函数改变时，粗损失下的源最优表示无法满足更精细目标损失的需求，并通过贝叶斯商形式化该障碍，在有限输出对数损失下得到量化恒等式，实验验证了分类等价表示在对数损失上的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迁移学习研究多关注分布偏移，但忽视了数据分布固定而损失函数变化时表示可能失效的情况。

Method: 引入贝叶斯商形式化损失的精炼关系，推导源最小表示用于更精细目标损失时的超额风险，并设计了控制实验、学习实验、合成图像和真实图像实验。

Result: 分类等价的表示在对数损失上表现不同，超量风险等于表示丢弃的关于Y的条件信息。

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Transfer learning is usually studied as a consequence of distribution shift. This paper identifies an orthogonal failure mode in which the data distribution is fixed and the loss changes. This setting is called \emph{loss shift}. A loss determines which information in \(X\) is Bayes-relevant, and two losses may therefore require different representations even under the same joint law \(P(X,Y)\). The idea is formalized using Bayes quotients, which allow losses to be ordered by refinement. In the Bayes-quotient formulation, strict refinement gives an immediate qualitative obstruction. A source-minimal representation for a coarser loss is insufficient for a strictly finer target loss. For finite-output log loss, this obstruction becomes an exact quantitative identity. The excess risk is the conditional information about \(Y\) discarded by the representation. Experiments in controlled, learned, synthetic-image, and real-image settings show the predicted effect, i.e., classification-equivalent representations can have different optimal log-loss performance under a fixed data distribution.

</details>


### [219] [From Uncertain Judgments to Calibrated Rankings: Conformal Elo Estimation for LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13221)
*Bora Kargi,David Salinas*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出SoftElo方法，通过局部不确定性传播和全局共形预测，在无需人工标注的情况下校准LLM评判的Elo评分，使其与人类评分误差降低至17.9 Elo MAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM作为裁判存在系统性偏差（如位置偏好、自偏好、非传递性），导致排名严重失准，而人工标注成本高昂。

Method: 局部层面：在Bradley-Terry过程中用校准胜率概率替代硬标签以传播不确定性；全局层面：对LLM与人类Elo残差施加分裂共形预测，生成无分布假设的预测区间。

Result: 在LMArena上55个留出模型上，LLM推导的Elo评分与人类评分的平均绝对误差为17.9，且预测区间提供有效的边际覆盖保证。

Conclusion: 该方法提供低成本、高可信度的模型评估工具，无需大规模人工标注即可获得校准的Elo估计和诚实的置信区间。

Abstract: Evaluating new large language models typically requires costly human annotation campaigns at scale. LLM-as-a-judge offers a cheaper alternative, but judge scores carry systematic errors - such as position bias, self-preference, or intransitivity - that can strongly miscalibrate the resulting rankings. We quantify the resulting judge-human disagreement at two complementary levels. At the local level, we estimate per-battle uncertainty from the judge's own score differences by propagating calibrated win probabilities rather than hard labels into the Bradley-Terry procedure. This alone provides a drastic improvement to Elo estimation accuracy, bringing LLM-derived ratings within 17.9 Elo MAE of human-derived ones when averaged over 55 held-out models on LMArena. At the global level, we apply split conformal prediction to the residual gap between LLM-derived and human-derived Elo ratings across held-out models, producing prediction intervals with distribution-free marginal coverage guarantees that account for irreducible LLM-human disagreement. Together, these two layers yield a low-cost evaluation tool that provides developers with calibrated Elo estimates and honest uncertainty bounds, without access to large-scale human annotations.To facilitate reproducibility, we release our code at https://github.com/kargibora/SoftElo .

</details>


### [220] [Extracting Governing Equations from Latent Dynamics via Multi-View Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13260)
*Paolo Muratore,Mackenzie Weygandt Mathis*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出DYSCO，一种多视图时间对比学习算法，能从高维噪声观测中联合恢复潜在轨迹和动力学，并通过符号恢复得到仿射规范下的控制方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从含噪高维观测中识别潜在动力系统是表示学习、系统辨识和科学发现的交叉难题，现有方法难以同时恢复轨迹与动力学。

Method: 利用多个独立噪声视图进行时间对比学习以分离信号与噪声，并用结构化函数基参数化动力学以实现符号恢复。

Result: 在混沌、振荡和亚稳态等动态系统及高斯/泊松噪声下，准确恢复潜在轨迹和流场，理论证明仿射模糊性下的可辨识性。

Conclusion: DYSCO有效解决了噪声非线性观测下的动力系统辨识问题，尤其适用于神经记录等应用场景。

Abstract: Identifying latent dynamical systems from noisy, high-dimensional measurements is a central problem at the intersection of representation learning, system identification, and scientific discovery. We present DYSCO, a multi-view temporal contrastive learning algorithm that jointly recovers latent trajectories and the governing dynamics from such observations, by leveraging multiple independent noisy views of the same underlying process to disentangle signal from noise. By parameterizing the dynamics in a structured functional basis, our framework further enables symbolic recovery of the governing equations within an affine gauge. We offer theoretical guarantees for strong identification up to an affine indeterminacy, extending prior identifiability results to the realistic setting of noisy nonlinear observations. Empirically, we demonstrate accurate recovery of both latent trajectories and flow fields across a diverse set of dynamical regimes (e.g., chaotic, oscillatory, and metastable) under both Gaussian and Poisson observation noise, the latter being particularly relevant for neural recordings.

</details>


### [221] [Once-for-All: Scalable Simultaneous Forecasting via Equilibrium State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13285)
*Beinan Xu,Andy Song,Jiti Gao,Feng Liu*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: ESE是一种同时预测多个交互系统的新范式，通过估计均衡状态与当前状态的差异生成整体预测，比现有方法更快、更可扩展，且保持高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法一次仅预测一个系统，难以高效处理多系统协同预测场景（如经济与医疗建模）。

Method: 先估计跨系统的均衡状态，再基于当前状态与均衡状态的差异生成整体预测，采用线性时间复杂度架构。

Result: 在合成与真实数据集（汇率、COVID-19传播）上，ESE至少与SOTA方法精度相当，但速度提升10-70倍，且在多系统扩展和扰动下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We introduce Equilibrium State Estimation (ESE), a novel paradigm for simultaneous prediction, where multiple interacting systems require separate yet coordinated forecasts. Such scenarios often arise in real-world settings such as economics and healthcare modeling. Unlike existing approaches that predict one system at a time, ESE forecasts all systems in a single pass. It first estimates the equilibrium state across systems, then generates holistic forecasts based on the difference between the current state and the estimated equilibrium. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets, including currency exchange and COVID-19 spread modeling, demonstrate that ESE is at least as accurate as state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods while being significantly faster. In addition, ESE integrates seamlessly with conventional predictors, combining their accuracy with its exceptional efficiency and delivering a 10-70x speedup. With linear-time complexity, ESE scales far better than SOTA methods as the number of systems increases. Moreover, it remains accurate under diverse perturbations, establishing ESE as a fast, generalizable, robust, and scalable multi-prediction method.

</details>


### [222] [Positional Encoding in the Context of Memristor-Based Analog Computation for Automatic Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13379)
*Benedikt Hilmes,Nick Rossenbach,Ralf Schlüter*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 通过调整忆阻器层中ADC的权重和精度位比例，可将执行退化相对减少约50%，在ADC不可修改时通过移除编码相关线性变换可减少约30%的退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 忆阻器计算因模拟-数字转换（ADC）中的输出值失真导致性能下降，特别是转换后的位置编码会产生大输出值，造成严重退化。

Method: 调整特定忆阻器层的ADC权重与精度位比例，以及移除编码相关的线性变换。

Result: 调整ADC参数后，执行退化相对减少约50%，且能耗稳定；在ADC不可修改时，退化减少约30%。

Conclusion: 通过优化ADC配置和编码处理，可有效减轻忆阻器计算中的失真问题，提升自然语言处理模型的资源效率。

Abstract: Memristors provide a new chance for resource-efficient computation of neural models for natural language processing by enabling analog execution of vector-matrix-multiplication. Yet, computations on these devices are currently subject to larger distortion, both in weight programming and execution. In this work, we identify large output values of transformed positional encodings to cause major degradation within analog-to-digital conversion (ADC) as part of memristor-based computation. By adjusting the proportion of weight and precision bits of the ADC of specific memristor layers, we reduce the degradation of the execution by ~50% relative, while keeping the estimated energy consumption stable. Additionally, we investigate scenarios where the ADC cannot be modified. In that case the degradation can be reduced by ~30% relative after removing encoding-related linear transformations.

</details>


### [223] [Uncertainty Estimation for Molecular Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13451)
*Paul Seij,Christian A. Naesseth,Stephan Mandt,Metod Jazbec*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一种基于拉普拉斯近似的后处理方法，在预训练的分子扩散模型中估计每个样本的不确定性，并通过过滤低质量样本来提升生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在3D分子生成中缺乏评估生成样本质量的先验信号，无法识别低质量样本。

Method: 对去噪网络进行拉普拉斯近似，测量生成轨迹中噪声预测的变异性，得到不确定性分数。

Result: 不确定性分数与样本质量指标呈负相关，可用于过滤低质量样本，在测试时通过缩放（scaling）提升模型性能。

Conclusion: 该不确定性估计方法提供了一种有效的后处理工具，能够提升分子扩散模型的生成质量。

Abstract: Diffusion models have seen wide adoption for 3D molecular generation, yet they offer no principled signal of when a generated molecule is likely to be of low quality. We propose a post-hoc method for estimating per-sample uncertainty in pretrained molecular diffusion models. Building on a Laplace approximation of the denoising network, we measure the variability of the noise prediction across the generation trajectory. Empirically, we show that the resulting uncertainty score is informative of sample quality, exhibiting a negative correlation with established sample-level quality metrics. We further study how the proposed uncertainty score can be used to filter generated samples, improving model performance via test-time scaling.

</details>


### [224] [MaxProof: Scaling Mathematical Proof with Generative-Verifier RL and Population-Level Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13473)
*Jiacheng Chen,Xinyu Zhang,Shunkai Zhang,Yanmohan Wang,Lin Li,Tiancheng Qin,Qin Wang,Zhengmao Zhu,Tianle Li,Jingyang Li,Zehan Li,Binyang Jiang,Jin Zhu,Han Ding,Fei Yu,Chenyu Du,Zijian Song,Jiayuan Song,Zhi Zhang,Yunan Huang,Weiyu Cheng,Pengyu Zhao,Yu Cheng*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: MaxProof是一个面向数学竞赛证明的群体测试时扩展框架，通过结合证明生成、验证和修复能力，在IMO 2025和USAMO 2026上超越了人类金牌阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在竞争级数学证明中缺乏有效的测试时扩展机制，难以在低假阳性率下实现高精度证明生成和筛选。

Method: 利用防御深度生成验证器训练三种面向证明的能力，并在测试时通过群体搜索和锦标赛选择进行证明的生成、验证、精炼和排序。

Result: M3模型在IMO 2025达到35/42，在USAMO 2026达到36/42，均超过人类金牌标准。

Conclusion: MaxProof通过测试时扩展显著提升了模型在数学竞赛证明任务上的性能，证明了群体搜索策略的有效性。

Abstract: We present MaxProof, a population-level test-time scaling framework for competition-level mathematical proof in the MiniMax-M3 series. M3 first trains three proof-oriented capabilities -- proof generation, proof verification, and critique-conditioned proof repair -- using a defense-in-depth generative verifier engineered for low false-positive rate. These capabilities are merged into a single released M3 model. At test time, MaxProof treats the model as a generator, verifier, refiner, and ranker, searches over a population of candidate proofs, and returns one final proof through tournament selection. With MaxProof test-time scaling, the M3 model reaches 35/42 on IMO 2025 and 36/42 on USAMO 2026, exceeding the human gold-medal threshold on both.

</details>


### [225] [CRAFTIIF: Cross-Resolution Analytic Four-Type Interpretable Isolation Forest for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13486)
*William Smits*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: CRAFTIIF是一种完全无监督的多变量时间序列异常检测框架，通过跨分辨率解析生成K=500个随机解析小波特征，覆盖四种异常类型（点异常、分布异常、时间异常、集体异常），并利用五个结构化隔离森林（每种类型一个加一个元IF）进行检测，使用自适应Otsu/MAD阈值校准，在mTSBench基准的19个数据集上平均F1=0.228，VUS-PR排名第一（0.463），优于先前最佳方法40.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列异常检测面临四种结构不同的异常类型（点、分布、时间、集体），每种需要不同的特征表示，但大多数无监督方法仅针对一两种类型且可解释性有限，缺乏全面覆盖所有类型的方法。

Method: CRAFTIIF通过跨分辨率解析生成K=500个随机解析小波特征（来自Morlet、DOG、Haar、Coiflet四个小波族），每种类型针对性设计特征，然后分别输入四个类型特定隔离森林和一个元隔离森林，利用自适应Otsu/MAD阈值自动校准检测率，无需数据集特定调整。

Result: 在mTSBench基准的19个数据集上，CRAFTIIF平均F1=0.228（全部19个）和F1=0.322（13个可检测数据集），VUS-PR达到0.463，相比先前最佳0.329提升40.7%，消融实验证实自适应阈值（+38%）、四分支结构（+20%）和元IF（+23%）均不可或缺。

Conclusion: CRAFTIIF通过全面覆盖四种异常类型和自适应阈值校准，显著超越了现有方法，同时提供直接异常类型归因，但诊断框架揭示了部分数据集本质不可检测，限制了上限潜力。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in multivariate time series is challenged by four structurally distinct anomaly types -- point (isolated spikes), distributional (level shifts), temporal (rhythm changes), and collective (inter-sensor correlation breakdowns) -- each requiring different feature representations. Most unsupervised methods target only one or two types and provide limited interpretability. We present CRAFTIIF (Cross-Resolution Analytic Four-Type Interpretable Isolation Forest), a fully unsupervised framework targeting all four types without dataset-specific tuning. CRAFTIIF generates K=500 random analytic wavelet feature draws across four families (Morlet, DOG, Haar, Coiflet), each targeting a specific anomaly type, feeding five structured Isolation Forests -- one per type plus a meta-IF for compound anomalies. An adaptive Otsu/MAD threshold calibrates detection automatically across anomaly rates from 0.1% to 69.2%. Because each IF is trained exclusively on type-specific features, branch firing provides direct anomaly-type attribution by construction, without post-hoc explanation. Evaluated on all 19 datasets of the mTSBench benchmark (Zhou et al., TMLR 2026), CRAFTIIF achieves mean F1=0.228 (all 19 datasets) and F1=0.322 (13 detectable datasets), ranking first among all 25 evaluated methods on VUS-PR (0.463 vs. previous best 0.329, +40.7%). A diagnostic framework -- oracle F1, detectability limits, and branch separation ratios -- identifies 6 of 19 datasets as fundamentally undetectable by any unsupervised method. Ablation over 11 conditions confirms adaptive thresholding (+38% F1), four-branch structure (+20%), and meta-IF (+23%) are each essential. Code: https://github.com/smitswil/craftiif

</details>


### [226] [Adjusted Cup-Product Neural Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13568)
*Snigdha Chandan Khilar*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一种硬编码杯积分的神经层，通过引入高阶规范理论的调整项实现规范不变性。理论证明闭合循环上输出仅取决于调整系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理与几何中许多可观测量是上链的杯积，现有方法无法保证规范不变性。

Method: 在杯积分神经层中加入调整项，使读出层在闭合循环上仅依赖调整系数。

Result: 当调整系数为零时输出完全消失；该可观测量为非零二次型，精确保持一重和二重规范变换下的不变性。

Conclusion: 调整项是规范不变信号的唯一来源，为物理和几何问题的神经网络建模提供了理论保证。

Abstract: Many important observables in physics and geometry are cup products of cochains. The adjusted cup product neural layer has been introduced in this paper. It is a neural primitive that hard wires the cup product with an adjustment term from higher gauge theory. This creates a readout that is gauge invariant by design. Their main theoretical result shows that on a closed cycle the output relies entirely on the adjustment coefficient. Setting this coefficient to zero removes the output completely regardless of other parameters. Thus the adjustment is the only source of gauge invariant signal. They prove this observable is a nonzero quadratic form and is exactly invariant under one and two gauge transformations.

</details>


### [227] [Learning with Simulators: No Regret in a Computationally Bounded World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13576)
*Sasha Voitovych,Abhishek Shetty,Noah Golowich,Alexander Rakhlin*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出可模拟过程框架，证明在仅访问数据生成过程模拟器时，能恢复与独立数据相同的VC维泛化保证，并给出同时学习所有有界多项式时间可采样过程的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习理论结果大多依赖数据独立性假设，对强依赖数据的研究有限，需探索更弱假设下的泛化条件。

Method: 引入可模拟过程框架，利用模拟器近似数据生成分布，通过条件采样建立统计与计算优势，结合时间有界柯尔莫哥洛夫复杂度设计通用算法。

Result: 在可模拟条件下恢复经典VC维错误界，并证明存在统一算法对所有多项式时间可采样过程实现受控遗憾。

Conclusion: 该框架显著拓展了经典PAC模型，表明在弱假设下仍可实现强学习保证，为依赖数据场景提供新理论工具。

Abstract: Understanding the minimal assumptions necessary for generalization is the fundamental question in learning theory. Unfortunately, most results rely heavily on independence (or some proxy thereof) of the data-generating process, while results for strongly dependent data are far more limited. Towards addressing this gap, we introduce the framework of simulatable processes, where the learner has access to a simulator that approximates the distribution generating the data (which may be an arbitrarily complex and dependent process). Surprisingly, given access to such a simulator, we show that we can recover the same learning guarantees as in the classical setting with independent data, namely, error bounds that depend on the VC dimension. Further, we use this framework to study the power of conditional sampling and show strict statistical and computational advantages in this setting. As a highlight of our framework, we exhibit a single algorithm that simultaneously learns any given VC class under all processes samplable in bounded polynomial time, with regret controlled by the time-bounded Kolmogorov complexity of the process. This provides a significant conceptual broadening of the classical PAC model.

</details>


### [228] [Understanding Truncated Positional Encodings for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13671)
*James Flora,Mitchell Black,Weng-Keen Wong,Amir Nayyeri*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 该研究首次系统分析了截断位置编码（如截断谱编码和行走编码）在图神经网络中的表达能力差异，发现截断后多种编码家族在理论上表达能力低于完整版本，且截断谱编码不再强于1-WL测试，实验表明混合截断编码更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际中广泛使用截断位置编码，但其理论性质未知，而完整版编码虽表达能力明确但计算复杂度过高。

Method: 理论分析截断谱编码、行走编码及k-调和距离的表达能力，通过比较它们与1-WL测试的关系证明差异，并结合真实数据集进行实验验证。

Result: 截断后不同编码家族表达能力根本不同；截断谱编码不再强于1-WL测试；混合截断编码在真实数据上表现优于单一家族。

Conclusion: 提醒研究者注意截断位置编码的理论局限性，并建议在实践中采用混合编码以提升性能。

Abstract: Positional encodings (PEs) enhance the power of graph neural networks (GNNs), both theoretically and empirically. Two of the most popular families of PEs - spectral (e.g., Laplacian eigenspaces, effective resistance) and walk-based (polynomials of the adjacency matrix) - are theoretically equivalent in expressive power, with expressivity between the 1-WL and 3-WL tests. However, this equivalence assumes the GNN uses the "complete" version of these PEs, which requires $O(n^3)$ time and space complexity. Instead, practitioners commonly use truncated variants of these encodings, such as the first $k$ eigenspaces or powers of the adjacency matrix. However, the theoretical properties of these truncated PEs are unknown. In this work, we initiate the study of these truncated PEs. Theoretically, we show that, under truncation, several families of PEs are fundamentally different in expressive power. As a corollary, we show that truncated spectral PEs are no longer stronger than the 1-WL test. We also study a family of spectral PEs, the $k$-harmonic distances, to highlight the differences in expressive power of even closely related truncated PEs. Finally, we experimentally show that a mix of truncated PEs is preferable to any single family on real-world datasets.

</details>
